# Backend test census and migration map

Checkpoint BO-M1-C0. The original I1 census is dated 2026-11-09; this document is reconciled to the I4 source tree dated 2026-11-10. The I1 note that rustfmt-only edits to 91 paths plus the cfg(unix) symlink gate were the only Rust source changes is historical. Later authorized iterations added the Mac-only fixture ignores, the Windows fail-closed migration/recovery guards, five test identities, the `configured_initial_branch` helper, and ten non-Windows gates. I4 moves the commit-exclusion regression into the existing service test suite without changing production code. The final source identities, platform gates, Windows listing, and I4 validation are recorded below; the authoritative working plan retains the iteration chronology.

## Repository pair and source identity

- Backend: bevy_openusd, branch develop/refactoring, HEAD 294d28e0134e4d18def81759820e88ad2a3c58eb. The I1 assignment-start tree was clean; I4 started from the existing dirty checkpoint tree and preserved it.
- Paired UI: UsdHubUI, branch refactoring, HEAD fa2faeef54e107cefbb0287ecf8830a0d45eba6f, read-only and clean at assignment start.
- The UI Cargo root resolves viewport_protocol, project_protocol, and usd_project through sibling paths under ../bevy_openusd. This records the exact local dependency pair; no UI build ran in C0.
- Cargo workspace resolver is 2. The root package is usdview; there are 11 member crates, 12 packages total. The optional xtra directory is excluded.

All files in all root/member src trees were enumerated: 827 files total, comprising 825 Rust files and two production WGSL shader files. The shaders are not test assets.

## Toolchain, targets and features

- Active toolchain: stable-x86_64-pc-windows-msvc; rustc 1.99.0 (b940084d7 2026-09-28); cargo 1.99.0 (5f94df478 2026-08-27).
- Installed targets: x86_64-pc-windows-msvc and wasm32-unknown-unknown. No repository rust-toolchain.toml or .cargo/config.toml was found; stable is the user's installed default.
- Root package default features are empty. Root features are solari (Bevy Solari) and dlss (Bevy anti-alias, DLSS and raw Vulkan init). Solari test modules under src/viewport/scene are feature-gated and absent from default-feature coverage. DLSS capability tests are under src/viewport/rendering/sampling.
- Inventory used the default Windows MSVC target and default features. No wasm test enumeration or feature-enabled test matrix was run. viewport_streaming has a macOS-specific encoder capability branch.

## Root tests directory: registered versus dormant

With autotests=false, only the ten explicit paths above are Cargo test targets. m10_persistent_soak_support.rs is included by m10_persistent_soak.rs. The remaining 37 files are present but neither target-registered nor module-included by a registered target. They are dormant source candidates, not current Cargo test identities. ## Source-tree test implementation and relocation map

The I1 path inventory remains the frozen comparison baseline: 224 test/helper/fixture-named paths beneath `src`, 18 additional files inherited under the cfg(test)-only OR8/M2 parent, and one file-backed cfg(test) module with a non-test filename (243 mapped source files/modules total). The source-tree scan counts overlap and must not be summed. For I4, a fresh source scan using `rg -o '#[test]'` over root `src`, every member-crate `src`, root `tests`, and all crate trees found 1,236 static `#[test]` attributes across the current workspace source/test tree; 1,086 are beneath `src` trees. A separate scan found 221 Rust source files with `cfg(test)` and 371 `cfg(test)` attributes. These are source identities, including platform/feature/dormant cases; they are not executed-test totals. The source identity appendix and completed Windows/default-feature listing below identify the current identities and explain their differences.

Destination rules:

- Root project modules map to tests/unit/project/<source-relative-path>; root viewport modules map to tests/unit/viewport/<source-relative-path>. Drop an existing internal tests path segment when moving into the new test hierarchy.
- Benchmark/performance/profile/soak modules map to tests/performance under the same domain path.
- Member crate modules map to crates/<crate>/tests/unit/<source-relative-path> or crates/<crate>/tests/performance/<source-relative-path>.
- Test-only helpers are colocated with their consuming test suite. A helper shared across suites is a later BO-M6 candidate for domain-owned testing support.
- Keep a minimal cfg(test) path child in the owning production module where needed to preserve privacy. Do not widen production visibility or protocol APIs. Public-surface integration tests remain normal Cargo test targets.
- These are proposed future destinations only. No source file moved in C0.

## cfg(test) module and helper relocation map

Inline module bodies are mapped to owner-relative files under the package test tree. A child module name other than tests is included in its proposed filename to prevent collisions. Their cfg(test) path hooks remain at the production owner where Rust privacy requires it. Test-gated helper functions move with the suite that consumes them. All destinations are proposals for later checkpoints; C0 moves no source.

## Fixture and support classification by consumer

- src/project/service/or8_m2/fixture.rs is a test harness builder, not production data. Its parent OR8/M2 subtree is included only under cfg(test); its consumers are the C1–C8 regression modules in that subtree.
- src/viewport/bim/test_fixtures.rs is declared cfg(test) in src/viewport/bim/mod.rs and supplies snapshot/entity builders to BIM classification, failure, index and unit tests.
- src/viewport/semantic/tests/fixtures.rs is declared by the test module tree in src/viewport/semantic/mod.rs and supplies response/snapshot builders to semantic unit modules.
- No production-consumed fixtures.rs file was found beneath any src tree. Performance scenario metadata in src/viewport/diagnostics/performance/scenario_definitions.rs is production diagnostics input, not test fixture data, and remains production.
- No dedicated mocks.rs or mocks/ directory exists under src. Inline fake/stub helpers are included in the test-module inventory and should move with their owner suite.
- Shared test data outside src: 41 root tests/stages USD files are root integration-test fixtures. crates/usd_semantic/tests/fixtures/identity_original.usda is included by usd_git tests and used by usd_semantic/tests/identity.rs; it is a cross-crate shared-fixture candidate for BO-M6/BO-M7. C0 leaves these files in place.

## Encapsulation and feature/platform constraints

- Historical I1 scan: 285 src-tree test-bearing files had `super::` references, with 940 of 1,081 I1 source test identities in those files. This is a conservative indicator, not a complete private-access proof; unqualified names can also resolve to private parent items. I4 does not use this heuristic to decide relocation; the current identity tables and module-owner constraints remain authoritative.
- Move implementations outside src, then keep minimal cfg(test) path child declarations where needed to preserve module ancestry. Do not widen pub, pub(crate), or protocol APIs for tests.
- Historical I1 static scan found 1,231 `#[test]` functions in the workspace source/test tree and 1,081 beneath `src` trees. The I4 rescan found 1,236 and 1,086 respectively. No `#[tokio::test]`, `rstest`, `test_case`, `proptest!`, or `quickcheck!` identities were found in the source census. Static identity tables below include platform- and feature-gated tests; the Windows Cargo listing is a separate target-specific enumeration.
- Solari cases require feature=solari. Root default features are empty. The wasm32 target was not used for test enumeration.
- The initial Windows enumeration and Compile attempt 02 reported E0433 in src/project/source_closure_tests.rs because std::os::unix::fs::symlink was imported unconditionally. Under a later Owner addendum, #[cfg(unix)] now gates both that import and the symlinked_source_is_rejected test. The Unix test name and source path remain; Windows excludes this Unix-only case.
- Separately, the concurrent build logged allocation failures and Windows paging-file OS error 1455 while mapping usd_bevy/openusd metadata, followed by delayed rustc ICE diagnostics and a 0xc0000409 exit in live_projection. These are recorded separately from the first target compile error; the log does not establish them as a cascade.

## Inventory enumeration and validation status

Historical I1 enumeration: `cargo test --workspace -- --list` ran from bevy_openusd before documentation edits and for inventory only. It exited 101, began compiling test targets, failed before emitting a completed list, and ran no test functions. The full log is `.agent/session_01a1bffd-69da-77b1-aa3e-106752a31c36/backend-test-list-enumeration.txt`; this is not a Formal Test receipt or PASS.

The first diagnostic is E0433 at log line 311. Lines 9532–9534 report Rust memory allocation failures; lines 9542, 9619 and 9629 report paging-file OS error 1455 while mapping metadata. Later delayed ICE/process-exit diagnostics are recorded separately above. At the time of enumeration, no correction had been authorized. A later Owner addendum authorized the cfg(unix) gate described above; it does not change this failed enumeration receipt.

Historical I1 post-fix enumeration used `cargo test --workspace -- --list` from bevy_openusd with `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1` scoped to that process. It exited 0 and captured the complete listing in `.agent/session_01a1bffd-69da-77b1-aa3e-106752a31c36/backend-test-list-postfix.txt` (573,350 bytes, SHA-256 `7a070c08f61373f22265eb1a6ee2c6ae3e14f53fd231a71ddf38be679f02b847`). That historical listing contained 1,963 unit/integration rows across 35 harnesses, 2 doctest rows across 12 sections, 1,177 distinct unit/integration names, and 786 duplicate harness occurrences. Its 54 static identities absent on Windows/default features were 45 identities across 37 dormant root test files, 8 Solari-gated identities, and one Unix-only symlink identity. Those counts describe I1 and are not reused for I4.

Current I4 enumeration used `cargo test --workspace -- --list` from `bevy_openusd` on the default Windows host target `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`, with workspace default features and `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1` scoped to the process. Cargo exited 0. The listing contained 1,953 unit/integration rows across 35 test harnesses and 2 doctest rows across 12 doctest sections, for 1,955 listing rows. There were 1,172 distinct unit/integration names and 781 duplicate harness occurrences; the repeated names are present once in the `usdview` library harness and once in the `usdview` binary harness. Each of the five I3 test identities appears twice in the Windows list. The 1,236 static `#[test]` identities reconcile to 1,172 Windows/default-feature unit/integration names plus 64 identities excluded from this target: 45 identities across 37 dormant root test files, 8 Solari-feature identities, one Unix-only symlink test, and ten non-Windows migration/cache success tests. The inventory command executed no test functions and is not a Formal Test result. No wasm or feature-enabled enumeration was run.

The Local Agent's I3 Formal Test receipt was PASS at source fingerprint `295b01fee20992d519ba478a669e6d7d1dd5f502d47af3b2d12992d6fe8a1928` (771 passed, 0 failed, 10 ignored). That receipt applies to I3 only and is not evidence for the I4 source after the test relocation. No full I4 Formal Test was run by the Implementer; the Local Agent owns that gate.

Configured Compile attempt 01 ran on 2026-11-09 and failed its first command, cargo fmt --all -- --check, before the workspace build. The Validation log and complete rustfmt location inventory are recorded in the final Compile result section.

## Baseline-aware migration progress report

The I1 C0 comparison baseline is preserved as historical data: 243 mapped test-only source files/modules under `src` (224 named paths plus 18 OR8/M2 inherited files and one non-test-named file-backed module), 121 inline cfg(test) module bodies, 16 cfg(test)-gated helpers, 47 path-attached cfg(test) module declarations, 186 other cfg(test)-gated items, and 1,081 static `#[test]` identities beneath `src`. The I1 categories overlap and are not a single total.

I4 final source-tree reconciliation: the 243 mapped source paths remain present and no new test-bearing production source path remains after moving the commit-exclusion regression body into the already-mapped `src/project/service/commit_tests.rs`. That existing test suite now retains identity `excluded_project_metadata_descendants_match_path_components`; no identity was removed. The current source scan found 1,086 static `#[test]` identities under `src` trees and 1,236 across `src`, `crates`, and root `tests`; this is a net +5 test identities from I1. Current source files contain 221 Rust files with `cfg(test)` and 371 `cfg(test)` attributes. The I3 helper `configured_initial_branch` is the seventeenth test-only helper when compared with the I1 helper inventory; its two consumers and private-access constraints are recorded above. The I4 commit test relocation removes the single I3-only inline module, leaving the 121 mapped I1 inline module bodies. The 47 path-attached cfg(test) module declarations and 186 other cfg(test)-gated items remain unchanged from I1; 121 + 17 + 47 + 186 reconciles to the 371 current cfg(test) attributes. These categories are source inventory counts, not executed-test totals.

The current Windows/default-feature Cargo list contains 1,172 distinct unit/integration test names; ten existing success identities remain in the source inventory and are intentionally excluded on Windows by `cfg(not(windows))`, not lost: `migrated_project_root_cache_contains_legacy_composed_content`; `interrupted_model_publication_preserves_the_legacy_wrapper`; `failed_rollback_preserves_transaction_for_a_later_attempt`; `committed_manifest_wins_over_a_stale_legacy_manifest_after_restart`; `invalid_canonical_manifest_preserves_recovery_transaction`; `missing_canonical_target_preserves_recovery_transaction`; `migrates_legacy_project_to_storage_v2_and_is_idempotent`; `migration_failure_after_ordinary_scene_restores_legacy_tree`; `migration_failure_before_manifest_restores_legacy_tree`; and `interrupted_migration_is_recovered_before_restart`. The five I3 regression identities are active on Windows, and the 45 dormant, 8 Solari-gated and 1 Unix-only identities remain distinct reasons for absent Windows listing names. For BO-M2–BO-M8, compare the current identities with the frozen C0 tables; track remaining baseline items, parity-evidenced moves and newly appearing source test paths separately. Preserve the no-new-violations rule before BO-M9; existing baseline debt remains counted until relocated. BO-M9 is the strict zero-src-test gate.

## Final Compile result

Configured Compile attempt 01 ran through Python 3.12 using AgentsComm/roles/local_agent_toolkit/scripts/Validation.py --layer compile. Validation.py returned exit code 10 after the first configured command failed: cargo fmt --all -- --check exited 1 in bevy_openusd. The runner stopped at that failure; cargo build --workspace --all-targets --locked did not run. This is a Compile FAIL, not a Test result.

The captured rustfmt output contains 787 diff-location headers across 91 unique Rust source files. The complete exact file:line list is .agent/session_01a1bffd-69da-77b1-aa3e-106752a31c36/backend-rustfmt-diff-locations.txt; the per-file summary is backend-rustfmt-diff-summary.txt. The immutable run-log copy is validation-compile-run01.log, SHA-256 b4fe3a38e078c8bc2fbe1294303b4dade807917f2a6c33a1e2df7415ed4a9526. The active log path at run time was AgentsComm/communication/agents/compile.log.

The first check did not modify Rust source. At the time of that attempt, the only backend worktree path was the new census document. After that run, the Owner authorized rustfmt-only formatting on the exact 91 unique Rust source paths listed in backend-rustfmt-target-files.txt. Rustfmt 1.10.0-stable ran with edition 2024 and child-module traversal disabled; the tracked diff contains exactly those 91 authorized paths, no outside-scope paths, and git diff --check passed. Compile attempt 02 passed cargo fmt --all -- --check, then failed cargo build --workspace --all-targets --locked (exit 101) with E0433 at src/project/source_closure_tests.rs:3. No memory allocation, paging-file, ICE, or 0xc0000409 messages appeared in attempt 02. A later Local Agent addendum authorized cfg(unix) only on the symlink import and symlinked_source_is_rejected test function; that exact test-only change is applied and the Windows import error is absent in attempt 03. Attempt 03 passed formatting but failed the workspace build: the earliest diagnostics concern a std metadata stub/prelude import and E0786 invalid openusd metadata; the E0786 note explicitly records failed mmap of libopenusd-089737aa1dd5d3f3.rlib with Windows OS error 1455. The same log separately reports 17 E0463 missing-crate errors, 225 E0433 missing-type errors later in the output, and rustc process exits 0xc0000409; the log does not establish all later diagnostics as a confirmed cascade. The preserved run03 log is validation-compile-run03.log (SHA-256 123fc9efdfbe0df70c1c2f072d801f0fcffdd0df428851fc179887695a6727b9). Compile attempt 04 ran with CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1 scoped to the Validation process; the variable was restored afterward and host/pagefile settings were unchanged. Validation.py returned exit 0, and both cargo fmt --all -- --check and cargo build --workspace --all-targets --locked passed. The run04 log copy is validation-compile-run04.log (361,442 bytes, SHA-256 933f1a89aa7181d11554ca5509fbd95c5ed94356b21e94f750f6681346068a17). Compile is PASS; Formal Test remains NOT_RUN.

The census was finalized with the post-fix enumeration result after Compile attempt 04. The final Validation Compile receipt for this updated census is recorded in the authoritative working plan.

## BO-M1-C1 root-package test classification

This section fulfills the requirements of **BO-M1-C1 — Inventory root tests** from `refactoring_plan_revised.md`.
Every root package (`usdview`) test identity has been inventoried and assigned to exactly one primary classification based on its assertions, fixtures, and execution boundaries.

### Classification Taxonomy
- `unit`: Isolated component algorithms, internal state transitions, or pure helpers without external I/O or multi-service cooperation.
- `integration`: Cooperation across real production components or service boundaries (e.g. `ProjectApplicationService`, `Git`, `LiveStage`, `Turso`, Bevy ECS).
- `contract`: Guarantees API wire protocols, serialization schemas, persistence migration versions, or external format stability.
- `acceptance`: End-to-end domain/user workflows judged against product acceptance criteria.
- `performance`: Benchmarks, frame-time budgets, memory allocations, profile captures, or soak behavior.

### Census Summary (Reconciled Static Inventory)
| Primary Classification | Active Windows Count | Gated / Dormant Count | Total Static Count | Percentage |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
